# Macete index by operator

Auto-generated by `(operator-index)`.  Inverted / browsing view: one section per mathematical operator, listing every result whose statement *mentions* it anywhere (hypothesis or conclusion), so "what do I know about X?" is a single lookup.  Logical glue (AND/OR/IMPLIES/IFF/=/quantifiers) is omitted.  311 operators over 2794 results.  Complements the head-keyed `MACETE-INDEX.md` (what each macete fires on).
